# Snippets for `Date` (M7 feature 12a), measured in Chromium 148 with
# tools/probes/js-date.json (made from this file by gen-date-cases.js). One
# snippet per line; `## NAME` starts a group that runs in every zone of the
# generator, `## NAME @ Zone, Zone` in those zones only. `!known ` marks a
# snippet where swb differs from Chromium on purpose (ADR 0026 section 15,
# 12a), `!later ` one that needs Chromium's other date forms or the long zone
# names (feature 12b); both go to the probe file and not to the swb script.
# A Date shows as `Date(milliseconds)`. No snippet depends on the current
# time. Large groups (the getters and the string forms over a list of
# instants, the local times around the transitions of each zone) were made
# by a short script from lists of instants; the lines are kept as made.

## toprimitive
Date.prototype[Symbol.toPrimitive].call({ toString() { return 's' }, valueOf() { return 'v' } }, 'default')
Date.prototype[Symbol.toPrimitive].call({ toString() { return 's' }, valueOf() { return 'v' } }, 'string')
Date.prototype[Symbol.toPrimitive].call({ toString() { return 's' }, valueOf() { return 'v' } }, 'number')
Date.prototype[Symbol.toPrimitive].call({ toString: 1, valueOf() { return 'v' } }, 'string')
Date.prototype[Symbol.toPrimitive].call({ toString() { return {} }, valueOf() { return 'v' } }, 'default')
